Scoreline and team-point validation

N189 evaluated 1,944 paired score configurations. The matched control uses the same team-score inputs and independent team scoring. Lower loss is better. Joint log loss scores the probability assigned to the actual final score. Team MAE measures average absolute point error. The 2026 column replays previously examined games after validation selections were locked.

Against its matched independent model, the 50/50 joint scoreline choice changed validation log loss by -0.00137. The descriptive 95% interval from 15,000 paired week resamples was -0.00315 to +0.00034, spanning zero. This interval excludes model-selection uncertainty.

N192 · GAME EVENTS

Forecasting close games, blowouts and scoring

Eight football outcomes have separate Brier and log-loss selections. Lower values are better. Game cards also show four complete probability ranges, each using one forecast for its low, middle and high outcomes.

Researchers completed 2,084 new validation fits and reused 28. The two improved 50/50 selections forecast a final margin of eight or fewer points.

2/16 primary selections and 10/80 selections across all five weightings improved on the best prior model in validation. Each validation season includes all 285 games and postseason. All 163 previously examined 2026 finals were graded after selection. The 2026 results did not determine the weights or the winners.

N191 · FORECAST UNCERTAINTY

How well do the ranges cover?

Interval scores reward narrow ranges and penalize misses. The weighted interval score (WIS) combines the median and the 50%, 80% and 90% ranges. Each individual band also has its own selection. Lower scores are better.

1 of 8 primary objectives and 7 of 40 weighted comparisons improved on the best standing control. The 50% margin interval scored 31.6895 against 31.7667. Both primary WIS choices retained existing forecasts. Validation covers 285 games each in 2024 and 2025, including postseason. The 163 completed 2026 games were graded after selection and had been examined previously.

The interval campaign completed 4,168 validation fits and reused 56. After selection it completed 13 fits, with 10 underlying base fits reported separately. Recalculating the seasonal ratio reused the same predictions.
